No changes have been made in the duties and powers of the bar, administrative and financial autonomy, and in the electoral process. Here, it will be determined by the regulation which will be issued by the Turkish Bar Association on the basis of equal and transformative representation of the Bars and on the basis of the deployment. If a bar president leaves before the election season is over, there will be no reelection on the bar board, and the bar board will elect its own bar president to complete the remaining deadline. With the clause proposal I have been promised, the bar president who left his post before the end of the election term should not be re-elected by the general council of the bar and the bar board will be able to elect its own bar president from among its members to complete the remaining time. However, the item, in effect, allows the president of the bar to re-elect as much as he wants. That's how it opens the way for people to get monopolized over the bar. This sentence, which can cause problems in the application, should be lifted instead, and should be selected for a four-year term, at most two times for the president of the bar. On the other hand, the second joke of Article 96 of the Law rules that the bar president will be senior for at least ten years. In this framework, if the elected bar president leaves his post, if there is not a ten-year-old lawyer on the board and, for example, a five-year senior lawyer is elected as the bar president, it would be against the Law of Law. For this reason, the requirement for vocational seniority of lawyers who will be elected bar president must be reduced from ten to five years; at the same time, young lawyers must be able to speak and adapt within the legislation. On the other hand, how to replace the member who left the board to be elected president has not been arranged. With the offer accepted, the offer will be legalized by the conflict within itself. Instead of a healthy, problem-solving law, there will be an arrangement that brings uncertainty. In addition, the chairmanship of the Bar and the membership of the Bar Board represent different authorities. We elected our Speaker last Tuesday. If our Speaker leaves his post, will he be elected president of the Presidential Court, or will he be elected president of the House?
